## US-0095 — Native in-Cursor `/auto` auto-chaining (no outer driver required)
- user_visible: true
- Title: Cursor-native continuous `/auto` — in-chat phase and backlog-drain chaining without `auto_outer_driver.py`
- Summary: Close the operator-experience gap left by **US-0092** / **DEC-0078** where **`AUTO_FLOW_MODE=full_autonomy`** + **`AUTO_BACKLOG_DRAIN=1`** still stops at **`stop_reason=completed (segment exhausted)`** after a single Cursor `/auto` turn and tells the operator to re-run `/auto` or **`python scripts/auto_outer_driver.py`**. Ship a **Cursor-native auto-chain** contract so one `/auto` orchestrator invocation **continues in-chat** across (1) **all intersected lifecycle phases** per **US-0088** reference Step 5, and (2) **backlog-drain segment boundaries** (next OPEN story/bug) **without** requiring the stdlib outer driver or manual re-invocation between segments — while preserving **spawn-only** orchestration (**BUG-0006** / **US-0069**), isolation (**US-0048**), strict proof (**US-0056**), and the **US-0088** / **US-0092** hard stop matrix. **`scripts/auto_outer_driver.py`** remains as an **optional** headless/CI fallback, not the primary IDE path when **`full_autonomy`** is enabled.
- Priority: P1
- Status: DONE
- Decomposition (US-0051):
  - **Single story (PO default)** — native in-chat chaining, drain-advance-without-pause in IDE, docs/runbook/contract-test updates, and outer-driver demotion to fallback ship as one vertical contract; partial delivery risks operators enabling **`full_autonomy`** without actual in-chat continuation.
  - **Rationale**: Shared stop matrix, spawn boundary, and operator messaging must stay coherent; splitting risks docs claiming native chaining while behavior still requires the outer driver.
- Overlap / duplicate evaluation:
  - **US-0092 (DONE) / DEC-0078**: shipped outer driver + **`full_autonomy`** — **US-0095 extends** with **primary IDE path** = in-chat auto-chain; does **not** remove outer driver or weaken spawn-only.
  - **US-0088 (DONE)**: continuous multi-phase + drain — **US-0095** closes the **Cursor single-turn** gap that **US-0088** documented as outer-driver equivalence (Option B).
  - **US-0044 (DONE)**: backlog drain — **US-0095** makes drain **in-chat** when **`full_autonomy`** enabled.
  - **US-0087 (DONE)**: bug-queue — composes; scheduler mutex unchanged.
  - **US-0023 (DONE)**: fresh subagent per phase — **compose**: auto-chain must **spawn** phase roles, not execute phases in orchestrator turn.
- Intake-time research (DEC-0011): **`R-0081`** (stub at intake — extend in **`/discovery`** / **`/research`**) — Cursor-native continuation model (orchestrator self-chain vs Task/subagent scheduling), segment drain advance in IDE, interaction with caps, outer-driver fallback boundary.
- Acceptance:
  - [x] AC-1: **`/auto`** command + **`auto-orchestration-reference.md`** define **native in-chat auto-chain** when **`AUTO_FLOW_MODE=full_autonomy`**: orchestrator **must not** stop after one phase or one story segment solely due to Cursor turn boundaries; continuation uses documented Cursor-native mechanism (e.g. orchestrator schedules next phase spawn or next drain segment **within the same `/auto` run**).
  - [x] AC-2: **Drain-without-pause (IDE)**: with **`full_autonomy`** + **`AUTO_BACKLOG_DRAIN=1`** (or bug-queue per **US-0087**), segment completion (**`refresh-context`** boundary) **immediately** schedules the next OPEN item **in-chat** — no operator message requiring **`auto_outer_driver.py`** or manual re-`/auto` for the default IDE recipe.
  - [x] AC-3: **Spawn-only preserved**: orchestrator turn still **must not** execute phase-role work (**BUG-0006**); each phase completes only via fresh subagent spawn + artifacts; contract tests regress **US-0069** preflight/post checks.
  - [x] AC-4: **Stop matrix unchanged for hard gates**: **`decision_gate`**, isolation/strict-proof violations, security deny, **`BACKLOG_MAX_STORIES_REACHED`**, **`AUTO_LOOP_MAX_CYCLES`**, unrecoverable **`error`**, **`pause_request`** — still hard stop in IDE path; relaxable transient stops per **US-0092** matrix when configured.
  - [x] AC-5: **Outer driver demoted to fallback**: runbook + README family state **`scripts/auto_outer_driver.py`** is **optional** for headless/CI/`--invoke-cmd` automation — **not** required for IDE **`full_autonomy`**; remove or rewrite prose that implies outer driver is mandatory for drain.
  - [x] AC-6: **Operator surface**: segment handoff may still notify when **`AUTO_QUIET=0`**; when **`AUTO_QUIET=1`**, suppress routine per-phase chatter but **do not** emit stop-and-wait instructions to run outer driver between segments under **`full_autonomy`**.
  - [x] AC-7: **DEC-0069 pairing**: every phase and drain boundary refreshes **`resume_brief`** + **`state.md`** before scheduling in-chat continuation; stale-brief fail-closed behavior unchanged.
  - [x] AC-8: **Contract tests** in **`tests/auto_command_contract_test.py`**: markers for native auto-chain, IDE drain-advance-without-outer-driver, spawn-only regression; template parity for touched surfaces.
  - [x] AC-9: **Template parity** (**US-0017**): active + **`template/`** mirrors for **`auto.md`**, reference excerpts, runbook subsection, scratchpad comments if touched.
  - [x] AC-10: **Caps + security**: **`AUTO_BACKLOG_MAX_STORIES`**, **`AUTO_LOOP_MAX_CYCLES`**, **`AUTO_BLOCK_RETRY_MAX`** honored in IDE chain; no auto-read **`.env`**, no intake evidence mutation, no publish without **`RELEASE_PUBLISH_MODE=auto`**.
- Boundaries:
  - In scope: Cursor IDE `/auto` in-chat chaining, IDE drain-advance, docs/tests/runbook, outer-driver fallback clarification.
  - Out of scope: removing all decision gates; bypassing QA/release/isolation/strict-proof; vendor guarantees beyond documented Cursor composition; deleting **`auto_outer_driver.py`**.
- related_us: US-0092, US-0088, US-0044, US-0087, US-0023, US-0069, US-0048, US-0056
- intake_evidence_ref: handoffs/intake_evidence/US-0095-intake-20260607.json
- intake_notes (2026-06-07, PO, `cursor-20260607-US0095-intake`, `INTAKE_GUIDED_MODE=1`, `INTAKE_WORK_ITEM_KIND=story`): **`/intake`** **PASS** — **`small-intake-pack`** selected (bounded orchestration refinement follow-up to **US-0092**; operator **`/ask`** thread on segment-exhausted stop despite **`full_autonomy`** + drain). All 5 required topics covered with distinct **`quoted_user_text`** per **BUG-0007** anti-echo rule. Validator: `python scripts/intake_evidence_validate.py --file handoffs/intake_evidence/US-0095-intake-20260607.json` → **`[INTAKE_EVIDENCE_VALIDATION_OK]`**. Duplicate analysis vs **US-0092** confirms distinct scope (native IDE chaining vs outer-driver bridge). Decomposition evaluator → **single story**. No decision gate. **Status: OPEN** per **US-0045**. **Next**: **`/discovery`** (fresh PO) for **`US-0095`**.
- discovery_notes (2026-06-07T18:30:00Z, PO, `orchestrator_run_id=auto-20260607-02`, `fresh_context_marker=po-US0095-discovery-20260607T183000Z-fresh`): **`/discovery`** **PASS** —
  - **Problem framing**: **US-0092** / **DEC-0078** shipped **`scripts/auto_outer_driver.py`** as the supported multi-turn bridge when Cursor ends an orchestrator turn early — operators enabling **`AUTO_FLOW_MODE=full_autonomy`** + **`AUTO_BACKLOG_DRAIN=1`** in the IDE still receive **`stop_reason=completed (segment exhausted)`** with prose directing them to re-run `/auto` or the outer driver. **US-0088** documented **Option B** outer-driver equivalence for this constraint; **US-0095** promotes a **primary IDE path**: one `/auto` invocation **self-chains in-chat** across intersected lifecycle phases (**reference Step 5**) and backlog-drain segment boundaries without mandatory outer driver or manual re-invocation between segments. **Spawn-only** (**BUG-0006** / **US-0069**), isolation (**US-0048**), strict proof (**US-0056**), and the **US-0088** / **US-0092** hard stop matrix remain unchanged.
  - **UX / operator flow (`AUTO_FLOW_MODE=full_autonomy`, IDE primary path)**:
    1. **Enable (default-off)**: operator sets **`AUTO_FLOW_MODE=full_autonomy`** in scratchpad; optionally **`AUTO_BACKLOG_DRAIN=1`** and/or bug-queue keys per **US-0087** scheduler mutex (unchanged).
    2. **Start (IDE)**: operator runs **`/auto`** once in Cursor — **not** `python scripts/auto_outer_driver.py` for the default hands-off recipe.
    3. **In-chat phase chain**: orchestrator spawns fresh phase-role subagent → phase completes via artifacts only → orchestrator runs **US-0069** preflight + **DEC-0038** strict-proof verification → **schedules next phase spawn within the same `/auto` orchestrator session** until intersected schedule exhausted or hard stop — orchestrator **must not** execute phase-role work in-band.
    4. **Segment boundary (drain-advance-without-pause)**: on **`refresh-context`** completion + drain policy, orchestrator reads segment boundary → selects next OPEN story/bug (compose **US-0044**, **US-0087**) → recomputes phase plan (**reference Step 5**) → refreshes **`resume_brief`** + **`state.md`** per **DEC-0069** → **immediately** continues in-chat to next segment's first phase — no operator message requiring outer driver or manual re-`/auto` under **`full_autonomy`**.
    5. **Operator surface**: when **`AUTO_QUIET=0`**, segment handoff may still notify; when **`AUTO_QUIET=1`**, suppress routine per-phase chatter but **never** emit stop-and-wait instructions to run outer driver between segments under **`full_autonomy`**.
    6. **Stop**: same hard gates as **US-0092** (`decision_gate`, isolation/strict-proof violations, security deny, **`BACKLOG_MAX_STORIES_REACHED`**, **`AUTO_LOOP_MAX_CYCLES`**, unrecoverable **`error`**, **`pause_request`**); relaxable transient stops per **US-0092** matrix when configured; caps honored in IDE chain.
    7. **Fallback (non-IDE)**: **`scripts/auto_outer_driver.py`** remains documented for headless/CI/`--invoke-cmd` automation — optional, not mandatory for IDE **`full_autonomy`**.
  - **Discovery-locked scope boundaries**:
    - **In**: `.cursor/commands/auto.md`, **`auto-orchestration-reference.md`**, **`architecture.md`** `# US-0095`, runbook subsection, **`resume_brief`** pairing contract, contract tests, operator messaging demotion of outer driver on IDE path.
    - **Out**: removing decision gates; bypassing QA/release/isolation/strict-proof; deleting **`auto_outer_driver.py`**; vendor guarantees beyond documented Cursor composition; weakening **BUG-0006** spawn-only.
  - **Assumptions**: Cursor `/auto` orchestrator can schedule **multiple sequential subagent spawns** (Task/subagent loop) within one orchestrator invocation without in-band phase execution; continuation is **orchestrator scheduling**, not orchestrator doing phase work; **US-0094** README intro outer-driver wording is a **follow-on doc touch** within US-0095 execute scope (demote to fallback, not delete autonomy headline).
  - **Risks**: **R1** Cursor turn/subagent depth limits block true in-chat chain → research must document bounds + fail-closed behavior; **R2** docs claim native chain while behavior still stops at segment exhausted → contract tests + operator-path grep for outer-driver mandatory phrases; **R3** spawn-only violation if continuation merges phase output into orchestrator turn → **US-0069** preflight/post checks regress; **R4** stale **`resume_brief`** between in-chat segment advances → **DEC-0069** fail-closed unchanged; **R5** dual-path operator confusion (IDE vs headless) → runbook must label primary vs fallback explicitly; **R6** cap desync between IDE chain counters and outer-driver ledger → research must unify accounting; **R7** partial delivery — single-story vertical contract mitigates.
  - **Research asks for R-0081 extension**:
    1. **Native continuation mechanism** — normative pattern for orchestrator self-chain within one `/auto` IDE invocation (sequential Task spawn loop vs documented alternative); interaction with Cursor turn boundaries; preserve **BUG-0006**.
    2. **IDE drain-advance algorithm** — deterministic segment-boundary read → next OPEN item selection → phase-plan recompute → in-chat resume without outer driver; compose **US-0044**, **US-0087**, **DEC-0069**.
    3. **Cap + ledger interaction** — **`AUTO_LOOP_MAX_CYCLES`**, **`AUTO_BACKLOG_MAX_STORIES`**, **`AUTO_BLOCK_RETRY_MAX`** accounting in IDE chain vs existing outer-driver / **`handoffs/auto_block_retry/`** ledger.

## US-0096 — Delivery modes: ultra-lean + mega-quick token lifecycle with layered memory
- user_visible: true
- Schema (optional per-row field when `AUTO_DELIVERY_ROUTING=backlog_then_scratchpad`):
  - `delivery_mode:` **`standard`** | **`ultra_lean`** | **`mega_quick`** (omit = resolver uses scratchpad / default **`standard`**)
- Title: Opt-in delivery modes — `DELIVERY_MODE` ultra_lean and mega_quick with layered memory, universal token wins, standard mode unchanged
- Summary: Close the token-cost gap left by **US-0080** / **DEC-0062** (context breadth via **`TOKEN_PROFILE`**) and **DEC-0052** (phase exclusion reinstatement blocks naive skips): ship a new **`DELIVERY_MODE`** scratchpad axis (**`standard`** \| **`ultra_lean`** \| **`mega_quick`**, default **`standard`**) that controls **lifecycle shape and artifact surface** while preserving near-same code quality. **`ultra_lean`** collapses eleven canonical phases into **four macro-phases** (`spec` → `plan` → `build+verify` loop → `ship`) with **`work/US-xxxx/pack.json`**, **`handoffs/active-context.md`**, and **section-scoped narrow-read** of vision/architecture/decisions — **not** amnesia. **`mega_quick`** routes **`/auto`** to enhanced **`/quick`** semantics for small work. **Tranche A** universal wins (narrow-read in all commands, tighter default hot surfaces, delta handoffs, touch-graph reads) ship **without** mode opt-in. **`DELIVERY_MODE=standard`** remains **byte-compatible** with today's full lifecycle, gates, and **DEC-0052** reinstatement.
- Priority: P1
- Status: DONE
- Decomposition (US-0051):
  - **Single story (PO default)** — shared **`DELIVERY_MODE`** contract, mode-scoped phase resolver, layered memory artifacts, universal wins, and docs/tests parity ship as one vertical slice; partial delivery risks operators enabling **`ultra_lean`** without memory index or **`mega_quick`** without test gates.
  - **Rationale**: Orthogonality rules (**`DELIVERY_MODE`** × **`TOKEN_PROFILE`** × **`CAVEMAN_MODE`**) and mode-scoped reinstatement must stay coherent; splitting risks docs claiming lean modes while **`/auto`** still materializes eleven phases.
- Overlap / duplicate evaluation:
  - **US-0080 (DONE) / DEC-0062**: token metrics + slim commands — **US-0096 extends** with **lifecycle-shape** axis; does not replace **`TOKEN_PROFILE`**.
  - **US-0053 (DONE) / DEC-0035**: narrow-read policy — **US-0096** extends to all phase commands + lean memory index.
  - **US-0070 (DONE) / DEC-0052**: phase selection — **US-0096** adds **mode-scoped** reinstatement (full chain only when **`DELIVERY_MODE=standard`**).
  - **US-0001 / `/quick`**: **US-0096** promotes **`/quick`** as **`mega_quick`** default path under **`/auto`**, not a replacement command.
  - **US-0092 / US-0095 (DONE)**: full_autonomy native chain — **composes**; lean modes reduce spawns per story; drain behavior unchanged.
- Intake-time research (DEC-0011): **`R-0082`** (stub at intake — extend in **`/discovery`** / **`/research`**).
- Acceptance:
  - [x] AC-1: **Scratchpad contract** — **`DELIVERY_MODE=standard|ultra_lean|mega_quick`** (default **`standard`**) documented in active + **`template/`** scratchpad surfaces; optional **`LEAN_MEMORY_READ`**, **`LEAN_MEMORY_WRITE`**, **`LEAN_COLD_READ_MAX_SECTIONS`**, **`LEAN_STATE_INDEX_ROWS`**, **`AUTO_DELIVERY_ROUTING`** keys documented; **non-substitution** paragraph: **`DELIVERY_MODE`** controls lifecycle shape/artifacts; **`TOKEN_PROFILE`** controls context breadth only; **`CAVEMAN_MODE`** controls reply voice only.
  - [x] AC-2: **`DELIVERY_MODE=standard` unchanged** — unset or **`standard`** materializes today's full canonical phase plan with **DEC-0052** default reinstatement, existing handoffs, sprint folder layout, isolation + strict-proof tuples, and release gates — contract tests regress no drift vs pre-**US-0096** baseline markers.
  - [x] AC-3: **Universal token wins (Tranche A)** — always-on improvements without mode toggle: narrow-read context packs in phase commands (beyond **`/ask`** only), stricter default hot-surface rollover thresholds or enforced caps, delta-style handoff append guidance, touch-graph read policy documented in runbook; measurable **`cache_read_tokens`** improvement on **`run_class_hash`-matched** standard runs per **DEC-0062**.
  - [x] AC-4: **`ultra_lean` macro-lifecycle** — when **`DELIVERY_MODE=ultra_lean`**, **`/auto`** materializes **`spec` → `plan` → `build+verify` → `ship`** (4 spawns + implementation loop inside **`build+verify`**); **no** reinstatement of eleven-phase chain; **`AUTO_IMPLEMENTATION_LOOP`** preserved inside **`build+verify`**; QA merges AC checklist + UAT in one spawn.
  - [x] AC-5: **Layered memory artifacts** — **`work/US-xxxx/pack.json`** (or equivalent canonical path) holds AC, tasks, refs, deltas, status; **`handoffs/active-context.md`** hot index (~30–80 lines, rolled over) lists read-before-code refs; each lean spawn reads index + pack + **section-scoped** vision/architecture/decision slices (cap **`LEAN_COLD_READ_MAX_SECTIONS`**); conditional writes append deltas to vision/architecture/decisions when new patterns learned.
  - [x] AC-6: **`mega_quick` mode** — when **`DELIVERY_MODE=mega_quick`**, **`/auto`** routes eligible work to enhanced **`/quick`** path: **`sprints/quick/Qxxxx/task.json`** + **`summary.md`** + tests + one **`state.md`** index row; AC in **`task.json`**; second spawn only on test failure; backlog status flip requires **`acceptance_met: true`** + green tests.
  - [x] AC-7: **Mode-scoped phase resolver** — **`/auto`** phase-plan materialization reads **`DELIVERY_MODE`** before **DEC-0052** reinstatement; reinstatement rules apply **only** when mode is **`standard`**; breadcrumbs record **`delivery_mode`**, **`resolved_phase_plan`**, **`memory_layer=pack|quick|standard`**.
  - [x] AC-8: **Optional backlog routing** — when **`AUTO_DELIVERY_ROUTING=backlog_then_scratchpad`**, story row may declare **`delivery_mode`** override; precedence: argv **`delivery-mode=`** → story row → scratchpad → **`standard`**.
  - [x] AC-9: **Quality floor (all lean modes)** — tests run before stop; AC traceability in pack or task.json; QA checklist row: if new pattern → architecture/decision delta written; **`active-context.md`** updated; institutional memory preserved for later runs.
  - [x] AC-10: **Contract tests + template parity** — **`tests/auto_command_contract_test.py`** markers for **`DELIVERY_MODE`** keys, mode-scoped reinstatement, **`pack.json`** schema, **`active-context.md`** contract, **`mega_quick`** routing literals; **`python scripts/check_intake_template_parity.py --scope=us-0096`** (or equivalent manifest scope) **PASS**; harness section registered.
  - [x] AC-11: **Architecture + decision lock** — **`decisions/DEC-xxxx.md`** + **`docs/engineering/architecture.md`** **`# US-0096`** document mode orthogonality, minimal quality invariants per mode, memory layer tiers (hot/warm/cold), and **AC-10** trade-offs; **`docs/engineering/runbook.md`** operator recipe (when to use each mode).
  - [x] AC-12: **Token-cost evidence** — lean-mode runs append **`handoffs/token_cost_runs/<orchestrator_run_id>.md`** rows with **`delivery_mode`** in run-class object; comparable **`ultra_lean`** vs **`standard`** runs document target reduction trajectory per **DEC-0062** (operator-run baseline acceptable for AC-12 quantitative claim).
- Boundaries:
  - In scope: **`DELIVERY_MODE`** axis, universal wins, **`ultra_lean`** + **`mega_quick`**, layered memory, mode-scoped resolver, docs/tests/parity, token-cost evidence fields.
  - Out of scope: removing **`standard`** lifecycle; bypassing tests in any mode; deleting existing handoff paths (remain for **`standard`**); vendor token guarantees; mandatory **`CAVEMAN_COMPRESS_INPUT`**; weakening **US-0039** release gates for **`standard`** mode.
- related_us: US-0080, US-0053, US-0070, US-0001, US-0092, US-0095, US-0048, US-0056, US-0069

## US-0097 — Project-owned root README bootstrap + per-story/sprint growth (framework README in `its_magic/` only)
- user_visible: true
- Title: Separate framework README from project README — bootstrap repo overview at project start and extend it on every shipped story/sprint
- Summary: Complete the **US-0062** / **DEC-0045** ownership split: **`its_magic/README.md`** is the only installer-delivered **framework** catalog; **root `README.md`** is **project-owned** and must be bootstrapped on first project story and extended on each shipped user story or sprint with a user-facing repo overview plus developer orientation. Refactor doc gates so **US-0091** static coverage applies to the **framework** README family (not the project root), and add a **project README** validator + **`/execute`** / **`/release`** mandatory steps (default-on) so operators no longer inherit a 1600-line its-magic README in new repos and never manually chase README drift.
- Priority: P1
- Status: DONE
- Decomposition (US-0051):
  - **Single story** — installer boundary, migration, bootstrap scaffold, phase wiring, gate separation, and validators ship as one vertical slice; partial delivery risks repos that stop shipping framework README to root but still lack project README automation.
  - **Rationale**: Operator pain spans install payload, lifecycle phases, and validation; splitting installer from execute gates would leave the observed gap (framework README at root, no growing project README) partially unresolved.
- Overlap / duplicate evaluation:
  - **US-0062 (DONE) / DEC-0045**: declared **`its_magic/README.md`** as framework surface — **US-0097 completes delivery** (root still receives framework README via manifest today).
  - **US-0091 (DONE) / DEC-0074**: framework feature coverage in README family — **distinct**: **US-0097** scopes **US-0091** to framework paths and adds **project** backlog ↔ root README coverage.
  - **US-0077 (DONE) / DEC-0059**: dual-audience **framework** docs — **compose**: project README uses a **simpler** user + developer section model for **product** content, not framework `USER_*`/`DEV_*` catalogs.
  - **US-0032 (DONE)**: optional per-feature guides at **`docs/user-guides/US-xxxx.md`** — **distinct** (optional, not root README); **US-0097** is default-on project overview at root.
  - **US-0030 (DONE)**: command/flag delta gate — **compose** unchanged for framework surfaces; project README gate is additive.
- Intake pack evidence (**first-intake-pack**, **US-0081** / **DEC-0064** plan map):
  - `selected_pack=first-intake-pack`
  - `asked_topics=` all eight first-intake keys covered
  - `missing_topics=(none)`
  - `assumptions_confirmed=(none)`
  - `plan_area_inventory` / `plan_area_coverage` / `coverage_complete=true` → all seven areas map to **`US-0097`**
  - `intake_evidence_ref=handoffs/intake_evidence/US-0097-intake-20260613.json`
- Acceptance:
  - [x] AC-1: **Installer ownership** — **`docs/engineering/context/installer-owned-paths.manifest`** no longer treats root **`README.md`** as framework install payload (framework catalog ships only under **`its_magic/README.md`** + **`template/its_magic/README.md`** parity); fresh **`missing`** install on an empty target does **not** lay down the its-magic framework README at repo root.
  - [x] AC-2: **Non-destructive migration** — **`upgrade`** / documented migration lifts legacy framework root **`README.md`** into **`its_magic/`** (or replaces root with a minimal project pointer) **without** deleting operator-authored project prose when detectable; deterministic reason codes + runbook remediation for ambiguous merges.
  - [x] AC-3: **Project README bootstrap** — on first **`/execute`** completion for a **project** story when root **`README.md`** is missing or matches a framework-placeholder sentinel, materialize a **project scaffold** (title from **`docs/product/vision.md`**, purpose blurb, empty feature catalog marker, user + developer sections).
  - [x] AC-4: **Per-story/sprint delta** — **`/execute`** (and **`/release`** when story ships) **requires** updating root **`README.md`** with at least one user-facing blurb for the target **`US-xxxx`** (and sprint summary cross-link when applicable); fail-closed reason family when delta skipped.
  - [x] AC-5: **Audience structure** — project README documents **(a)** end-user repo overview (what the product does, how to run/use) and **(b)** developer orientation (setup, layout, where code/tests live); framework commands catalog remains only in **`its_magic/README.md`** (optional one-line pointer from project README allowed).
  - [x] AC-6: **Gate separation** — **`validate_readme_feature_coverage.py`** ( **US-0091** ) validates **framework** README paths only; new **`validate_project_readme_coverage.py`** (name locked in architecture) validates **project** DONE **`user_visible`** backlog rows against root **`README.md`**; umbrella + sub-codes documented.
  - [x] AC-7: **Release composition** — **`/release`** runs project README gate (scratchpad **`PROJECT_README_ENFORCE=1`** default after bootstrap) alongside existing framework gates; **`PROJECT_README_ENFORCE=0`** records skipped evidence during migration only.
  - [x] AC-8: **Metadata hygiene** — project README edits pass **`check-user-visible-metadata.py`** (**US-0071**); no internal planning tokens in operator-facing project blurbs.
  - [x] AC-9: **Contract tests + template parity** — harness markers for installer manifest, migration sentinel, execute/release mandatory steps, and project validator; **`check_intake_template_parity.py --scope=project-readme`** (or equivalent manifest) **PASS**.
  - [x] AC-10: **Architecture + runbook** — **`decisions/DEC-xxxx.md`** + **`docs/engineering/architecture.md`** **`# US-0097`** document ownership matrix, placeholder detection, tranche order (A installer → B bootstrap → C phase wiring → D validators), and operator troubleshooting; active + **`template/`** command/runbook parity.
- Boundaries:
  - In scope: installer/manifest, migration, project README bootstrap + deltas, phase command wiring, project validator + release gate, framework/project gate separation, tests/parity/runbook.
  - Out of scope: rewriting **US-0094** framework intro; replacing **`docs/product/`** as canonical backlog; mandatory regeneration of historical framework README catalog in **`its_magic/`** beyond parity moves; per-feature **`docs/user-guides/`** (**US-0032**) behavior changes.
- related_us: US-0062, US-0091, US-0077, US-0032, US-0030, US-0071, US-0017
- intake_evidence_ref: handoffs/intake_evidence/US-0097-intake-20260613.json

## US-0098 — Dev environment auto-launch profile (detect, persist, relaunch, connect)
- user_visible: true
- Title: Auto-detect developer runtime, persist profile, rebuild/relaunch after execute changes, and surface connection parameters
- Summary: Close the gap where **US-0065** / **US-0086** / **US-0067** validate or route at **workflow phase boundaries** but do **not** continuously **rebuild, restart, and reconnect** the app in the **detected dev environment** after implementation changes. Ship a **scratchpad-gated dev auto-launch profile** (default-off) so **`/execute`** (and optional explicit operator refresh) can: **(1)** detect **local**, **docker-host-local** (shell/`docker` on the same machine), or **automation remote** targets; **(2)** **persist** an operator-seeded **dev-environment profile** the agent reuses; **(3)** run **bounded** container rebuild / process restart recipes; **(4)** present **operator-ready connection info** (URL/host:port, health path, service/container id) with **names-only** secret refs — composing with **US-0064**, **US-0085**, **US-0086**, and existing **`DEV_SERVER_*`** scratchpad keys without reading **`.env`**.
- Priority: P1
- Status: DONE
- Decomposition (US-0051):
  - **Single story** — profile schema, detection heuristics, execute relaunch contract, container orchestration, operator connection surface, and docs/tests ship as one vertical slice; partial delivery risks persisted profiles without safe relaunch or relaunch without connection reporting.
  - **Rationale**: Operator pain spans detection, persistence, rebuild, and UX; splitting profile from relaunch would leave “AI remembers env but never restarts” partially unresolved.
- Overlap / duplicate evaluation:
  - **US-0065 (DONE) / DEC-0047**: phase-gated runtime QA startup — **distinct**: **US-0098** targets **dev-loop relaunch during execute**, not only QA/verify verdict evidence.
  - **US-0086 (DONE)**: automation remote **test routing** — **compose**: shared heuristics; **US-0098** adds **dev profile persistence + relaunch + connection surfacing**, not **`TEST_COMMAND`** reroute alone.
  - **US-0067 (DONE) / DEC-0049**: release **Run/Connect/Verify** hints — **distinct**: **US-0098** surfaces connection info **during development**, not at release finalization.
  - **US-0085 (DONE) / DEC-0071**: **`.env`** exclusion — **US-0098** must inherit (no AI read; env-ref names only in persisted profile).
  - **US-0064 (DONE)**: connectivity schema unchanged — **US-0098** maps to existing target ids / **`*Env`** indirection.
  - **US-0093 (DONE)**: **`process_health`** / **`DEV_SERVER_COMMAND`** — **compose**: relaunch may feed probes; **US-0098** owns profile + rebuild orchestration.
- Intake pack evidence (**first-intake-pack**, **US-0081** / **DEC-0064** plan map):
  - `selected_pack=first-intake-pack`
  - `asked_topics=` all eight first-intake keys covered
  - `missing_topics=(none)`
  - `assumptions_confirmed=(none)`
  - `plan_area_inventory` / `plan_area_coverage` / `coverage_complete=true` → all eight areas map to **`US-0098`**
  - `intake_evidence_ref=handoffs/intake_evidence/US-0098-intake-20260613.json`
- Acceptance:
  - [x] AC-1: **Scratchpad + `template/`** define an explicit **dev auto-launch profile** (architecture-locked flag names, default **off**); when **off**, manual daily dev workflows remain **unchanged** (no mandatory relaunch overhead).
  - [x] AC-2: **Dev-environment profile schema** — committed template + operator-writable persisted profile (path **architecture-locked**, e.g. **`.cursor/dev-environment.json`**) with **names-only** secret refs; operator may seed once; agent updates idempotently with evidence; **no** inline secrets in git-tracked JSON.
  - [x] AC-3: **Environment detection** — deterministic labels at minimum **`local`**, **`docker-host-local`**, **`docker`**, **`ssh`** (or architecture-locked synonym table); **docker-host-local** = direct shell/`docker`/`docker compose` on the dev machine (not remote SSH); fail-closed reason codes when profile/stack unresolved.
  - [x] AC-4: **`/execute` relaunch contract** — when dev auto-launch profile is **on** and implementation touches documented runtime/container surfaces, run **bounded** rebuild/restart (e.g. **`docker compose build`** + **`up`**, or stack-aware dev server restart); record relaunch evidence in **`handoffs/dev_to_qa.md`** (command, mode, outcome, retry count).
  - [x] AC-5: **Operator connection surface** — after successful relaunch, emit deterministic **Connect** block for the operator (URL or **`host:port`**, health check path, service/container/target id, runtime mode); **no** secret values; align field shapes with **`runtime-connectivity.md`** where applicable.
  - [x] AC-6: **Composition** — reuse **US-0086** routing precedence where automation remote is enabled; inherit **US-0085** **`.env`** exclusion; extend **`DEV_SERVER_COMMAND`** / **`DEV_SERVER_PORT`** semantics documented in scratchpad; do **not** replace **US-0064** JSON schema.
  - [x] AC-7: **Explicit refresh path** — document operator phrase and/or command hook (architecture-locked) to **refresh dev environment** without waiting for execute task completion; fail-closed when profile disabled or target unroutable.
  - [x] AC-8: **Bounded safety** — retry/relaunch caps, non-transient fail-fast (e.g. missing compose file, disabled target), deterministic reason-code family (e.g. **`DEV_ENV_PROFILE_*`**, **`DEV_ENV_RELAUNCH_*`**); no unbounded file-watch daemon in v1 unless architecture documents a safe bounded trigger.
  - [x] AC-9: **Contract tests + template parity** — stdlib helper self-test (if shipped), command/runbook markers, **`check_intake_template_parity.py --scope=dev-environment`** (or equivalent manifest) **PASS**.
  - [x] AC-10: **Architecture + runbook** — **`decisions/DEC-xxxx.md`** + **`docs/engineering/architecture.md`** **`# US-0098`** document profile schema, detection matrix, execute relaunch triggers, docker-host-local vs remote semantics, troubleshooting; active + **`template/`** parity.
- Boundaries:
  - In scope: dev profile schema/persistence, detection heuristics, execute-phase relaunch, container rebuild recipes, operator connection surfacing, scratchpad gates, optional stdlib helper, docs/tests/parity.
  - Out of scope: replacing **US-0064** schema; production deploy/release orchestration; unsupervised infinite watch loops; reading **`.env`**; mandating remote setup for manual daily work.

## US-0099 — Auto-bootstrap dev-environment profile on install/upgrade (non-destructive)
- user_visible: true
- Title: Copy dev-environment profile example to local path when missing after install/upgrade
- Summary: Close the operator gap after **US-0098** / **DEC-0084** where scratchpad **`DEV_ENVIRONMENT_CONFIG`** points to **`.cursor/dev-environment.json`** but **install/upgrade** does not create the local profile — forcing a manual **`Copy-Item template/.cursor/dev-environment.json.example …`** before **`DEV_AUTO_LAUNCH_PROFILE=deterministic_v1`** can load the profile (**`DEV_ENV_PROFILE_MISSING`**). Ship **non-destructive auto-bootstrap**: on **`missing`**, **`upgrade`**, and **npm `postinstall`** paths, copy **`template/.cursor/dev-environment.json.example`** → resolved profile path (default **`.cursor/dev-environment.json`**, override via merged scratchpad **`DEV_ENVIRONMENT_CONFIG`**) **only when the target file is absent** — **never** overwrite operator-customized profiles. Update runbook so seeding is **customize-after-bootstrap** (not prerequisite). Composes with **US-0018** smart upgrade, **US-0085** names-only example, and **DEC-0084** gitignore posture unchanged.
- Priority: P2
- Status: DONE
- Decomposition (US-0051):
  - **Single story** — installer/postinstall bootstrap, path resolution, skip-if-exists semantics, docs/tests/parity ship as one vertical slice; partial delivery risks copy-on-install without upgrade parity or overwrite regressions.
  - **Rationale**: Operator pain is entirely at install/upgrade boundary; splitting installer from npm postinstall would leave **`npx its-magic`** consumers still missing the file.
- Overlap / duplicate evaluation:
  - **US-0098 (DONE) / DEC-0084**: defined schema + operator-seeded persistence — **US-0099 completes delivery** with first-run bootstrap; does **not** change profile schema v1 or execute step **24** semantics.
  - **US-0018 (DONE)**: smart upgrade — **compose**: bootstrap hook runs during **`upgrade`** without clobbering local files.
  - **US-0085 (DONE)**: **`.env`** exclusion — **compose**: copied example remains names-only; no secret materialization.
  - **US-0062 (DONE)**: installer manifest — **distinct**: optional manifest row for example source only; local profile stays gitignored.
- Intake pack evidence (**small-intake-pack**):
  - `selected_pack=small-intake-pack`
  - `asked_topics=` all five small-pack keys covered
  - `missing_topics=(none)`
  - `assumptions_confirmed=(none)`
  - `intake_evidence_ref=handoffs/intake_evidence/US-0099-intake-20260614.json`
- Acceptance:
  - [x] AC-1: **Copy-when-missing** — **`installer.py`** / **`installer.ps1`** / **`installer.sh`** **`missing`** and **`upgrade`** modes copy **`template/.cursor/dev-environment.json.example`** → resolved profile path when target absent; emit deterministic log line (names-only).
  - [x] AC-2: **Never overwrite** — when profile file already exists at resolved path, installer **skips** copy; reason family includes **`DEV_ENV_BOOTSTRAP_SKIPPED_EXISTS`** (or architecture-locked synonym); operator prose unchanged on disk.
  - [x] AC-3: **Path resolution** — resolved path = merged scratchpad **`DEV_ENVIRONMENT_CONFIG`** when parseable, else default **`.cursor/dev-environment.json`**; relative to repo root; fail-closed **`DEV_ENV_BOOTSTRAP_PATH_INVALID`** when override malformed.
  - [x] AC-4: **npm postinstall parity** — **`bin/postinstall.js`** (or documented equivalent) performs same copy-when-missing for **`npx its-magic`** / **`npm install its-magic`** consumers; idempotent on re-run.
  - [x] AC-5: **Example source contract** — committed **`template/.cursor/dev-environment.json.example`** remains schema v1 names-only (**US-0085**); local **`.cursor/dev-environment.json`** stays in **`.gitignore`** + **`.cursorignore`** (unchanged from **DEC-0084**).
  - [x] AC-6: **Runbook + operator UX** — **`docs/engineering/runbook.md`** § dev environment updates: bootstrap is automatic on install/upgrade; manual copy is **customize** step only; troubleshooting for **`DEV_ENV_PROFILE_MISSING`** references bootstrap path.
  - [x] AC-7: **Contract tests + parity** — **`test_us0099_*`** markers (copy-when-missing, skip-when-exists, upgrade idempotency, path override); extend **`DEV_ENVIRONMENT_PAIRS`** / **`check_intake_template_parity.py --scope=dev-environment`** when installer surfaces touched.
  - [x] AC-8: **Architecture + decision** — **`decisions/DEC-xxxx.md`** amends **DEC-0084** bootstrap posture; **`docs/engineering/architecture.md`** **`# US-0099`** documents hook placement and reason codes; active + **`template/`** parity.
- Boundaries:
  - In scope: install/upgrade/postinstall bootstrap, path resolution, skip-if-exists, runbook, tests/parity, decision/architecture notes.
  - Out of scope: changing **DEC-0084** profile schema; auto-detecting compose services; enabling **`DEV_AUTO_LAUNCH_PROFILE`** by default; reading **`.env`** to populate connect fields.

## US-0100 — Version-scoped release changelog and GitHub release-note attachment
- user_visible: true
- Title: Maintain cumulative version changelog plus per-version release docs with GitHub/git publish integration
- Summary: Close the operator gap where **US-0040** sprint-scoped notes (`handoffs/releases/Sxxxx-release-notes.md`) and **`handoffs/release_queue.md`** track workflow releases but there is **no semver-aligned, growing changelog** listing **US-xxxx** / **BUG-xxxx** with short descriptions per version, and **`scripts/release-all.sh`** still uses **`gh release create --generate-notes`** instead of canonical its-magic release bodies. Ship **version-scoped release documentation**: a **cumulative** artifact (Keep a Changelog–style **`CHANGELOG.md`** or equivalent canonical path) that grows with each published version, **per-version** release note files keyed by semver/git tag, and **`/release`** finalization that **derives** entries from sprint release notes + backlog summaries, binds **`release_version`** on queue rows, and supplies the body for **GitHub Releases** / git tag publish paths — composing with **US-0040**, **US-0054** / **`RELEASE_PUBLISH_MODE`**, and existing **`release-targets.json`** without bypassing confirmation gates.
- Priority: P1
- Status: DONE
- Decomposition (US-0051):
  - **Single story** — cumulative changelog, per-version docs, `/release` derivation hook, queue version binding, GitHub/git attach step, backfill, and validators ship as one integrated contract; splitting would leave semver docs disconnected from sprint notes or publish paths still using auto-generated GitHub bodies.
  - **Rationale**: Operator value is "one truthful release story" across repo docs, GitHub Releases, and npm/choco/brew publish; partial delivery (docs only without publish hook) fails the stated GitHub/git integration goal.
  - **Split axes considered**: (1) docs-only vs publish integration — rejected (user explicitly requested GitHub/git best-practice wiring); (2) backfill vs forward-only — forward-only rejected (existing **~80+** released sprint notes would leave cumulative history empty).
- Overlap / duplicate evaluation:
  - **US-0040 (DONE)**: per-sprint notes + queue — **compose**: **US-0100** adds **version** layer; does **not** replace sprint-scoped notes or overwrite non-target sprints.
  - **US-0067 (DONE)**: Run/Connect/Verify operator hints in sprint notes — **compose**: version docs summarize **US/BUG** work items; operator hints remain in sprint notes with cross-link.
  - **US-0054 (DONE) / DEC-0036**: publish confirmation — **compose**: GitHub body attachment runs under same **`RELEASE_PUBLISH_MODE`** posture; no auto-publish without explicit mode.
  - **US-0008 / release-all.sh (DONE)**: unified npm/choco/brew + GitHub tag — **extend**: replace **`--generate-notes`** with canonical notes file when version docs exist; fail-safe fallback documented.
  - **US-0091 (DONE)**: README feature coverage gate — **distinct**: version changelog is release artifact family, not README catalog parity (may cross-link only).
- Intake pack evidence (**first-intake-pack**):
  - `selected_pack=first-intake-pack`
  - `asked_topics=` all eight first-pack keys covered
  - `missing_topics=(none)`
  - `assumptions_confirmed=(none)`
  - `plan_area_inventory=` 7 areas (`version-changelog-artifacts`, `release-phase-integration`, `github-git-publish`, `release-queue-version-binding`, `publish-targets-compose`, `backfill-migration`, `validators-tests-parity`)
  - `plan_area_coverage=` all areas → **`US-0100`**; `coverage_complete=true`
  - `intake_evidence_ref=handoffs/intake_evidence/US-0100-intake-20260615.json`
- Acceptance:
  - [x] AC-1: **Canonical cumulative changelog** — define and document a growing version history artifact (recommended: repo-root **`CHANGELOG.md`** following [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/) 1.1.0 shape: **`[Unreleased]`** + semver sections newest-first) listing each released version with **US-xxxx** / **BUG-xxxx** ids and **one-line summaries** sourced from backlog titles/summaries or sprint note **What's new** bullets.
  - [x] AC-2: **Per-version release docs** — define canonical per-version paths (recommended: **`handoffs/releases/vX.Y.Z-release-notes.md`** or documented equivalent) containing the semver release body used for operator + GitHub consumption; must not overwrite unrelated version files.
  - [x] AC-3: **`/release` derivation hook** — on successful release finalization (after **US-0039** gates), **`/release`** appends/updates cumulative changelog + per-version doc for the target sprint's **`release_version`** (semver from **`package.json`** or explicit queue field when publish bump occurs); idempotent re-run must not duplicate version sections.
  - [x] AC-4: **`release_queue` version binding** — **`handoffs/release_queue.md`** rows populate **`release_version`** deterministically when finalized; changelog entries cross-reference **`sprint_id`**, **`story_refs`**, and **`handoffs/releases/Sxxxx-release-notes.md`**.
  - [x] AC-5: **GitHub / git publish attachment** — **`scripts/release-all.sh`** (and **`/release`** publish path when **`RELEASE_PUBLISH_MODE`** allows) uses **`gh release create … -F <canonical-version-notes>`** (or equivalent) instead of **`--generate-notes`** when version docs exist; documents fallback when **`gh`** absent or notes file missing (**fail-closed** or explicit skip reason code per **US-0054**).
  - [x] AC-6: **Backfill contract** — provide deterministic one-time/idempotent backfill from existing **`released`** queue rows + **`handoffs/releases/Sxxxx-release-notes.md`** to seed cumulative history (grouped by best-effort semver when **`release_version`** was unset — document operator remediation for ambiguous rows).
  - [x] AC-7: **Validator + reason codes** — Python helper/validator (e.g. **`scripts/release_changelog_validate.py`**) checks changelog ordering, required US/BUG refs for finalized versions, queue/changelog consistency; fail-closed family includes **`RELEASE_CHANGELOG_VERSION_MISSING`**, **`RELEASE_CHANGELOG_DUPLICATE_VERSION`**, **`RELEASE_CHANGELOG_WORK_ITEM_GAP`** with remediation text.
  - [x] AC-8: **Runbook + command docs** — **`docs/engineering/runbook.md`** and **`.cursor/commands/release.md`** document version-doc touchpoints, GitHub release body source-of-truth, and operator workflow (local **`/release`** vs **`release-all.sh`** vs CI tag push).
  - [x] AC-9: **Contract tests + template parity** — **`test_us0100_*`** markers in **`tests/auto_command_contract_test.py`**; **`check_intake_template_parity.py`** scope when release command/template surfaces touched; active + **`template/`** behavioral alignment.
  - [x] AC-10: **Architecture + decision anchor** — **`decisions/DEC-xxxx.md`** locks artifact paths, derivation precedence (sprint notes → backlog → queue), and publish compose rules with **US-0040** / **US-0054**; **`docs/engineering/architecture.md`** **`# US-0100`** appended.
- Boundaries:
  - In scope: version changelog artifacts, per-version docs, `/release` + publish integration, queue version field population, backfill, validators, runbook/command updates, tests/parity.
  - Out of scope: replacing sprint-scoped **`Sxxxx-release-notes.md`**; auto-bumping semver without operator/`release-all.sh` intent; GitHub **`.github/release.yml`** label taxonomy (optional future); rewriting historical sprint narratives.

## US-0101 — Per-phase model tier selection for subagents (MODEL_TIER + local catalog)
- user_visible: true
- Title: Configure LLM model strength per its-magic phase/role via stable tiers and optional local slug catalog
- Summary: Close the operator gap where **all** subagent phases inherit one parent model or ad-hoc **`model:`** slugs in **`.cursor/agents/`** — with no framework contract for **cheap vs strong** models per lifecycle phase, no protection against **volatile model IDs** in template files, and no documented **API-only / BYOK** caveats when Cursor subagents may not route through custom API keys. Ship **`MODEL_TIER`** scratchpad controls (**`cheap` \| `balanced` \| `strong`**) mapped to Cursor-stable aliases (**`fast`**, **`inherit`**, optional slug from operator-local catalog), a **default phase→tier matrix** for **`/auto`** spawn paths, **template agent defaults** using aliases only (no hardcoded vendor slugs in **`template/`**), optional **`.cursor/model-catalog.local.json`** (gitignored) for operator-maintained slug maps, and runbook guidance for **`MODEL_PROVIDER_MODE=cursor|api`** — **orthogonal** to **`TOKEN_PROFILE`** (**DEC-0062** / **US-0080**: context breadth / token cost only, never model choice).
- Priority: P2
- Status: DONE
- Decomposition (US-0051):
  - **Single story** — scratchpad tier contract, default matrix, agent template defaults, local catalog schema, resolver/materializer hook, provider-mode runbook, validators, and contract tests ship as one vertical slice; splitting would leave tiers documented without agent application or catalog without fallback semantics.
  - **Rationale**: Operator value is "light phases stay cheap, coding phases stay strong" across **`/auto`** spawns without re-editing framework files on every Cursor model rename.
  - **Split axes considered**: (1) docs-only tier matrix vs agent **`model:`** application — rejected (docs alone do not change spawn behavior); (2) dynamic **`models.list()`** at IDE spawn — deferred (SDK/automation path documented; IDE subagents use aliases + local catalog only in v1).
- Overlap / duplicate evaluation:
  - **US-0003 (DONE)**: subagent definitions — **extend**: add **`model:`** tier defaults to **`.cursor/agents/*.mdc`**.
  - **US-0080 / DEC-0062 (DONE)**: **`TOKEN_PROFILE`** — **orthogonal**: **`MODEL_TIER`** selects LLM strength; **`TOKEN_PROFILE`** selects context-pack breadth only; docs must state non-substitution explicitly.
  - **US-0069 / DEC-0051 (DONE)**: phase→role matrix — **compose**: tier keys align to canonical **`phase_id`** list used by **`/auto`**.
  - **US-0023 / US-0048 (DONE)**: fresh subagent isolation — **unchanged**: tier selection must not weaken spawn-only or isolation evidence gates.
  - **US-0092 / DEC-0078 (DONE)**: outer driver / SDK agents — **distinct**: document optional per-send **`model`** for programmatic paths; out of scope for mandatory IDE resolver v1.
- Intake pack evidence (**small-intake-pack**):
  - `selected_pack=small-intake-pack`
  - `asked_topics=` all five small-pack keys covered
  - `missing_topics=(none)`
  - `assumptions_confirmed=(none)`

- Acceptance:

## US-0102 — Direct per-phase model slug override and role-based catalog presets (US-0101 extension)
- user_visible: true
- Title: Extend US-0101 tier-based model selection with direct per-phase slug override and role-based catalog presets, while retaining 3-tier baseline
- Summary: US-0101 delivered per-phase `MODEL_TIER_<PHASE>` (cheap/balanced/strong) → Cursor alias mapping (fast/inherit) with optional local catalog for vendor slugs. Operators now request a second axis: **direct per-phase model slug assignment** (`MODEL_<PHASE>=<vendor-slug>`) that bypasses the tier indirection layer, plus **role-based catalog presets** (PO/SA/DEV/QA model profiles per `ai_modell_auslegung_cursor_highend.md`) for operators who want curated model sets without manual tier mapping. The 3-tier system remains as the default/fallback layer: **precedence** `MODEL_<PHASE>` > `MODEL_TIER_<PHASE>` > `MODEL_TIER_DEFAULT` > Cursor alias (local catalog or stable alias). The `/ask` phase (already in US-0101 default matrix as `cheap`) is reinforced with direct override support. **Compose with US-0101/DEC-0086** (do not amend — US-0101 stays DONE). **Backward compatible**: tier-only configurations continue to work unchanged. **No volatile vendor IDs in template files** — direct slug keys and role-based presets live in local catalog JSON (gitignored) only; templates keep Cursor-stable identifiers. New catalog schema v2 extends v1 with optional `roles` section. New contract tests `test_us0102_*` validate precedence, schema, backward compatibility.
- Priority: P2
- Status: DONE
- Decomposition (US-0051):
  - **Single story** — direct override scratchpad keys, precedence logic, catalog schema v2, role-based presets, backward compatibility, validator updates, contract tests ship as one vertical slice; splitting would ship override logic without catalog integration or reverse.
  - **Rationale**: "tier system stays default; advanced operators can assign exact models per phase or pick a role-based preset" without breaking US-0101 DONE status or forcing tier users to migrate.
- Overlap / duplicate evaluation:
  - **US-0101 / DEC-0086 (DONE)**: per-phase tier selection — **compose, do not amend**: US-0101 stays DONE; US-0102 adds a second overlay axis (direct slug override + role-based catalog). Precedence documented: `MODEL_<PHASE>` > `MODEL_TIER_<PHASE>` > `MODEL_TIER_DEFAULT` > Cursor alias. No changes to US-0101 ACs or DEC-0086 locks.
  - **US-0080 / DEC-0062 (DONE)**: `TOKEN_PROFILE` — **orthogonal**: direct slug override still selects LLM identity (not context breadth); non-substitution paragraph in runbook.
  - **US-0003 (DONE)**: subagent definitions — **unchanged**: template agents keep Cursor-stable aliases; direct slugs and role presets live in local catalog only (no volatile vendor IDs in templates).
  - **US-0092 / DEC-0078 (DONE)**: outer driver / SDK agents — **distinct**: document optional per-send `model` for programmatic paths; out of scope for mandatory IDE resolver v1.
  - **US-0023 / US-0048 (DONE)**: fresh subagent isolation — **unchanged**: direct slug override must not weaken spawn-only or isolation evidence gates.
- Intake pack evidence (**small-intake-pack**):
  - `selected_pack=small-intake-pack` (bounded refinement of US-0101; clear scope expansion)
  - `asked_topics=` all five small-pack keys covered
  - `missing_topics=(none)`
  - `assumptions_confirmed=(none)`
  - `intake_evidence_ref=handoffs/intake_evidence/US-0102-intake-20260624.json`
- Acceptance:
  - [x] AC-1: **Direct per-phase slug override scratchpad keys** — document `MODEL_<PHASE>=<vendor-slug>` keys (canonical phase ids: same as US-0101 list, including `ask`). Values are direct vendor model slugs (e.g. `MODEL_EXECUTE=kimi-k2.7-code`). **Forbidden** in `template/.cursor/scratchpad.md`: hardcoded vendor slugs (document only in `.cursor/scratchpad.local.md` examples). Merge precedence for model resolution: `MODEL_<PHASE>` > `MODEL_TIER_<PHASE>` > `MODEL_TIER_DEFAULT` > Cursor alias (local catalog or stable alias).
  - [x] AC-2: **Precedence validation and resolution logic** — `scripts/model_tier_lib.py` (or new `scripts/model_overrides_lib.py`) implements deterministic resolution: (1) check `MODEL_<PHASE>` scratchpad key → return slug or fail-closed `MODEL_OVERRIDE_SLUG_UNKNOWN`; (2) fall back to `MODEL_TIER_<PHASE>` tier → resolve via US-0101 chain; (3) fall back to `MODEL_TIER_DEFAULT` tier → resolve; (4) fall back to Cursor stable alias per US-0101 AC-3. Unknown slug in step (1) → `MODEL_OVERRIDE_SLUG_UNKNOWN` reason code with remediation text.
  - [x] AC-3: **Local catalog schema v2 with role-based presets** — extend `.cursor/model-catalog.local.json` schema from v1 to v2: add optional `roles` section alongside existing `tiers`. Schema: `{schema_version: 2, tiers: {cheap, balanced, strong}, roles: {po, sa, dev, dev_difficult, qa, security, release}, notes}`. Role slugs follow `ai_modell_auslegung_cursor_highend.md` recommendations. **Backward compatible**: v1 catalogs (no `roles`) still work; v2 catalogs are opt-in. New example catalogs: `.cursor/model-catalog.local.example.role-based-balanced.json`, `.cursor/model-catalog.local.example.role-based-highend.json` (placeholder slugs only).
  - [x] AC-4: **Role-based resolver (opt-in)** — scratchpad flag `MODEL_RESOLVE=alias_only|local_catalog|role_catalog` (default `alias_only`). When `role_catalog`, resolver looks up phase → role mapping (from `AUTO_ROLE_RESEARCH`, `AUTO_ROLE_PLAN_VERIFY`, or phase→role defaults per `US-0069 / DEC-0051`) → role slug from catalog. Precedence: `MODEL_<PHASE>` > `MODEL_TIER_<PHASE>` > `role_catalog` role lookup > `MODEL_TIER_DEFAULT` > Cursor alias. Role lookup fails → `MODEL_ROLE_SLUG_UNKNOWN` → fall back to tier or alias.
  - [x] AC-5: **/ask phase reinforcement** — `/ask` phase already in US-0101 default matrix as `cheap` tier; reinforce with direct override support: `MODEL_ASK=<slug>` scratchpad key works same as other phases. No change to default matrix (keep `ask: cheap`); no change to US-0101 AC-1 (phase list already includes `ask`).
  - [x] AC-6: **Backward compatibility** — existing US-0101 configurations (tier-only, no direct overrides, v1 catalogs) continue to work unchanged. No migration required. Validator accepts both v1 and v2 catalog schemas. Contract tests verify tier-only resolution still produces correct aliases without direct override keys present.
  - [x] AC-7: **Template stability and volatile-ID protection** — `template/.cursor/scratchpad.md` examples show only `MODEL_TIER_*` keys (no `MODEL_<PHASE>` vendor slugs). `template/.cursor/model-catalog.local.example.json` and role-based examples use placeholder slugs (`<your-po-model-slug>`, not `glm-5.2` directly). Operator configures slugs in `.cursor/scratchpad.local.md` and `.cursor/model-catalog.local.json` only (gitignored). Forbidden: hardcoded vendor slugs in `template/` files (grep check in contract tests).
  - [x] AC-8: **Validator + reason codes** — `scripts/model_tier_lib.py` extends to validate: (1) direct slug keys (`MODEL_<PHASE>=<slug>`) for valid phase ids and non-empty slugs; (2) catalog schema v2 (roles section optional, but when present must have valid slugs); (3) precedence resolution logic; (4) backward compatibility (v1 catalogs). New fail-closed reason codes: `MODEL_OVERRIDE_SLUG_UNKNOWN` (direct slug not recognized), `MODEL_ROLE_SLUG_UNKNOWN` (role catalog lookup failed), `MODEL_CATALOG_SCHEMA_V2_INVALID` (v2 schema validation failure). Extend existing `MODEL_TIER_*` reason codes to handle v2 catalogs.
  - [x] AC-9: **Contract tests + template parity** — `test_us0102_*` markers validate: (1) direct slug scratchpad keys (`test_us0102_direct_override_keys`), (2) precedence resolution (`test_us0102_precedence_chain`), (3) catalog schema v2 (`test_us0102_catalog_schema_v2`), (4) role-based resolver (`test_us0102_role_catalog_resolver`), (5) backward compatibility (`test_us0102_tier_only_backward_compat`), (6) template stability (`test_us0102_no_vendor_slugs_in_template`), (7) reason code inventory (`test_us0102_reason_codes`), (8) `/ask` phase reinforcement (`test_us0102_ask_phase_reinforcement`). **`check_intake_template_parity.py --scope=model-tier-overrides`** added to existing `--scope=model-tier` family.
  - [x] AC-10: **Documentation + runbook** — `.cursor/scratchpad.md` documents `MODEL_<PHASE>` keys with examples in comments (no vendor slugs). Runbook and architecture `# US-0102` documents direct override precedence, role-based resolution, backward compatibility. `ai_modell_auslegung_cursor_highend.md` referenced in runbook as role-based recommendation source (not normative).
- Boundaries:
  - In scope: direct per-phase model slug scratchpad keys, role-based catalog presets, `/ask` phase reinforcement, backward compatibility for tier system.
  - Out of scope: changing US-0101 DONE status or DEC-0086 locks; requiring migration from tier-based to direct override; forcing operators to use role-based catalogs; modifying Cursor billing-plan enforcement; fixing Cursor subagent BYOK bugs (document only); changing mandatory QA/release gate semantics; TOKEN_PROFILE behavior changes.

## US-0103 — AI Decision Ledger + Plan Fidelity policy
- user_visible: false
- Title: Append-only ledger of every AI deviation and plan-fidelity scratchpad tri-state
- Summary: Sovereign-loop foundation - every AI decision made while running autonomously must be auditable. New handoffs/sovereign_decisions/<orchestrator_run_id>.jsonl records each decision as {ts, role, decision_id, type, from_artifact, to_artifact, rationale, plan_fidelity, cross_model_reviewed, risk_tier}. Governed by AUTO_PLAN_FIDELITY=strict|relaxed|extended: strict = hard stop on deviation, relaxed = AI may drop/reorder ACs, extended = AI may add new stories/features. QA cross-checks the ledger at every /qa boundary. Default-off AI_DECISION_LEDGER=0|1. Compose with US-0070/US-0048/US-0069 (do not amend).
- Priority: P0
- Status: OPEN
- Decomposition (US-0051):
  - **Single story** - audit ledger + plan-fidelity policy are one vertical capability; splitting would orphan the ledger or the governance.
  - **Rationale**: "Every autonomous AI decision must be recorded and auditable; plan-fidelity governs what deviations are allowed."
- Overlap / duplicate evaluation:
  - **US-0070 (DONE)** / **US-0048 (DONE)** / **US-0069 (DONE)** / **US-0092 (DONE)**: phase selection policy, isolation evidence, phase role enforcement, full-autonomy outer driver - **compose, do not amend**: sovereign loop adds ledger + plan-fidelity governance ON TOP of resolved_phase_plan.

## US-0104 — Cross-Model Adversarial Critic
- user_visible: false
- Title: Per-phase cross-model critic subagent with Challenger/Architect/Subtractor lenses + anti-slop scoring
- Summary: After each phase-role subagent completes, sovereign loop optionally spawns a critic subagent using a different model from the producer. The critic evaluates through one of three lenses: Challenger (edge cases, race conditions), Architect (coupling, boundaries), Subtractor (over-engineering, premature abstraction). Findings reconcile via parallel jury: agreement = high-confidence; single-finder = flagged. New field model_id in isolation evidence. Anti-slop scoring (per-lens 0-N score; below threshold results in rework loop). Falls back to single-model-multi-lens when only one model CLI installed. Compose with US-0048/US-0069/US-0110.
- Priority: P1
- Status: OPEN
- Acceptance (8 ACs): CROSS_MODEL_REVIEW scratchpad flag, critic subagent spawn via /sovereign-critic command, model_id in isolation evidence, reconciliation via sovereign_critic_lib.py with confidence levels, anti-slop scoring with threshold + rework loop, degraded single-model fallback, contract tests, documentation + template parity.
- Boundaries:
  - In scope: critic subagent spawn, three-lens prompt, reconciliation logic, anti-slop scoring, degraded fallback, contract tests, template parity.
  - Out of scope: changing US-0048/US-0069 isolation/role semantics; mandatory QA/release gate weakening; US-0101/US-0102 tier resolution changes.
- related_us: US-0048, US-0069, US-0023, US-0101, US-0102, US-0103, US-0110
- intake_evidence_ref: handoffs/intake_evidence/intake-sovereign-20260627-01.json

## US-0108 — Parallel Instance Arbitrage for dev phase
- user_visible: false
- Title: Multiple dev subagents race on same task in isolated worktrees; QA critic picks winner
- Summary: Under SOVEREIGN_PARALLEL_DEV=1, execute spawns N (bounded, default 3) parallel dev subagents in isolated git worktrees with different models/lenses. QA cross-reviewer evaluates all N outputs; passing one selected (first PASS + highest anti-slop score; ties break by earliest proof_issued_at). Merge via AUTO_SOVEREIGN_MERGE_RESOLVE=first_pass_wins|last_pass_wins|manual. Resource guard AUTO_SOVEREIGN_PARALLEL_MAX_TOTAL=6. Compose with US-0047/US-0092.
- Priority: P2
- Status: OPEN
- Acceptance (8 ACs): SOVEREIGN_PARALLEL_DEV scratchpad flag with N parameter, worktree isolation per subagent bounded by AUTO_SOVEREIGN_WORKTREE_MAX, selection predicate (first PASS + highest anti-slop), merge policy (first_pass_wins|last_pass_wins|manual), resource guard with cap, contract tests, backward compat (0 = single dev), documentation + template parity.
- Boundaries:
  - In scope: parallel dev scratchpad flags, worktree isolation logic, selection predicate, merge policy, resource guard, contract tests, template parity.
  - Out of scope: changing US-0047/US-0092 semantics; mandatory QA/release gate weakening; bulk execute changes.

## Bug issues (canonical)

Per **`DEC-0061`** / **`US-0079`**: defect work items use **`BUG-####`** ids (**allocator**: next id after highest existing in this section), **`OPEN`/`DONE`** only, and required fields **`environment`**, **`steps_to_reproduce`**, **`expected`**, **`actual`**, **`evidence_refs`** (non-empty). Append new bugs as **`### BUG-#### — Title`** blocks; keep blocks **sorted by id**. Optional link bullets: **`related_us`**, **`blocks_us`**, **`duplicate_of`**, **`supersedes`** (ids only).

**Validator**: `python scripts/bug_issue_validate.py --backlog docs/product/backlog.md` — pass **`--check-acceptance`** to reconcile **`docs/product/acceptance.md`** **`## Bug acceptance (canonical)`** rows vs backlog status.

**Routing**: merged scratchpad **`INTAKE_WORK_ITEM_KIND=bug`** and/or explicit **`/intake bug`** before persisting a defect (**`DEC-0061`** §5); run `python scripts/intake_bug_routing_guard.py --kind story --file <prose.txt>` to catch defect-shaped prose without bug signals (fail **`INTAKE_BUG_ROUTING_REQUIRED`**).

### BUG-0002 — map-codebase does not write codebase-map in fresh repos
- user_visible: true
- Status: DONE
- environment: Freshly installed `its-magic` repos (reported from test repos on Windows 10); command path `/map-codebase`; expected artifact `docs/engineering/codebase-map.md`.
- steps_to_reproduce: 1. Initialize a new repo and install `its-magic`. 2. Run `/map-codebase` in the fresh repo. 3. Observe that `docs/engineering/codebase-map.md` is not created/updated.
- expected: Agents should have a reliable codebase map in fresh repos without relying on user memory of manual bootstrap steps.
- actual: Current expectation was mismatched: `/map-codebase` is explicit/manual; automatic lifecycle ownership for map creation is not clearly enforced.
- evidence_refs: user clarification (2026-03-31); `.cursor/commands/map-codebase.md`; `template/.cursor/commands/map-codebase.md`; `handoffs/intake_evidence/BUG-0002-intake-20260331.json`; `handoffs/intake_evidence/US-0082-intake-20260331.json`.
- discovery_notes (2026-03-31, PO, manual): Reclassified from defect to enhancement request. Track implementation under `US-0082` (agent-driven codebase map bootstrap). Close `BUG-0002` as expectation mismatch to avoid duplicate tracking.
- related_us: US-0001, US-0082

### BUG-0003 — Missing scripts still occur on install modes missing/upgrade
- user_visible: true
- Status: DONE
- environment: Fresh installs and upgrades in test repos (`its-magic`), reported for install modes `missing` and `upgrade`; parity expected across `installer.ps1`, `installer.sh`, `installer.py`.
- steps_to_reproduce: 1. Install into a repo using mode `missing` or run `upgrade`. 2. Inspect installed script set. 3. Observe required scripts are still missing after install/upgrade.
- expected: Install/upgrade produce complete required framework script set for the selected version; no missing framework-critical scripts after successful run, including `enforce-triad-hot-surface.py`.
- actual: Required scripts are still missing in `missing`/`upgrade` scenarios; reported concrete miss: `scripts/enforce-triad-hot-surface.py` absent after install in a new repo.
- evidence_refs: user reports (2026-03-31); `installer.ps1`; `installer.sh`; `installer.py`; `template/scripts/`; `scripts/enforce-triad-hot-surface.py`; `docs/engineering/context/installer-owned-paths.manifest`; `handoffs/intake_evidence/BUG-0003-intake-20260331.json`; `handoffs/intake_evidence/BUG-0003-intake-20260331-b.json`; related prior issue `BUG-0001`.
- discovery_notes (2026-03-31, PO, manual): This is treated as a regression/remaining-gap follow-up to `BUG-0001` scope, focused on mode-specific install completeness (`missing` + `upgrade`). Addendum: triad gate script specifically reported missing after fresh install. Next: `/discovery` to isolate per-mode copy/skip logic and required script inventory contract.
- discovery_notes (2026-03-31T21:42:38Z, PO, `orchestrator_run_id=auto-20260331-03`): `/discovery` confirmed this is not a duplicate close of `BUG-0001` but a mode-path completeness gap that can persist in `missing`/`upgrade` branches even when baseline payload exists; overlap retained as `duplicate_of: BUG-0001` for lineage only. Research readiness is now explicit: isolate per-mode copy/skip predicates in `installer.ps1`/`installer.sh`/`installer.py`, define a deterministic required-script inventory contract (including `scripts/enforce-triad-hot-surface.py`), and lock parity/regression evidence expectations before architecture.
- research_notes (2026-03-31T21:44:46Z, tech-lead, `orchestrator_run_id=auto-20260331-03`): Added `R-0061` with branch-logic inventory for `missing`/`upgrade` across `installer.ps1`/`installer.sh`/`installer.py`. ### BUG-0004 — installer.sh fails in shell path with `set: Illegal option -`
- user_visible: true
- Status: DONE
- environment: Linux shell session (`root@docker-dmz:/workdir/dev_git/ai_docker_manager`), npm-global runtime path `/usr/lib/node_modules/its-magic/installer.sh`, command `its-magic --mode missing`.
- steps_to_reproduce: 1. Open shell in a Linux repo. 2. Run `its-magic --mode missing`. 3. Observe immediate failure with `/usr/lib/node_modules/its-magic/installer.sh: 2: set: Illegal option -`.
- expected: Installer invocation for mode `missing` runs successfully in supported shell environments and does not fail at startup due to unsupported shell option flags.
- actual: Installer exits immediately on line 2 with `set: Illegal option -`, blocking install flow.
- evidence_refs: user report and terminal output (2026-04-03); `handoffs/intake_evidence/BUG-0004-intake-20260403.json`; `installer.sh`; CLI entrypoint path `its-magic --mode missing`.
- intake_notes (2026-04-03, PO, manual): Captured as new shell-compatibility defect from production-like run (`root@docker-dmz`), separate from script-completeness issues in `BUG-0003`. Preserve bug routing (`/intake bug`) and prioritize deterministic shell/runtime compatibility check in discovery.

Next recommended phase: `/research` to lock bounded fix options and regression matrix.

### BUG-0005 — `/auto` fails with stale resume target after bug intake
- user_visible: true
- Status: DONE
- environment: `its-magic` workflow in this repo after canonical bug intake for `BUG-0004`; command path `/auto` (no explicit `start-from`); `handoffs/resume_brief.md` still points to pre-intake `intake` target.
- steps_to_reproduce: 1. Run `/intake bug` and persist `BUG-0004`. 2. Run `/auto` without `start-from`. 3. Observe immediate fail-fast `AUTO_RESUME_ERROR` due to stale resume target (`RESUME_BRIEF_STALE`, `resume target says intake`).
- expected: After successful bug intake persistence, `/auto` should resolve a valid next phase for the newly created/open bug context (for example `discovery`) or deterministically self-heal resume breadcrumbs without requiring manual override for normal continuation.

### BUG-0006 — `/auto` executes phases without spawning required subagents
- user_visible: true
- Status: DONE
- sprint_id: S0067
- environment: `/auto` orchestration path in this repository with canonical phase lifecycle execution enabled; concern applies to phase dispatch behavior whenever orchestrator can run phase work directly instead of spawning required role subagents.

### BUG-0007 — intake evidence records asked questions that were never asked
- user_visible: true
- Status: DONE
- sprint_id: S0068
- environment: `/intake bug` flow in this repository; generated intake evidence artifact shows `asked_topics` and `topic_coverage` answers although the user reports no questions were asked in-chat.
- steps_to_reproduce: 1. Run `/intake bug` with a short bug report. 2. Inspect generated intake evidence file. 3. Compare `asked_topics` and `topic_coverage[].quoted_user_text` against actual chat turns; observe answered topics recorded without corresponding prompted questions.
- expected: Intake evidence must only claim questions/topics as asked when they were actually asked (or explicitly marked as delegation/equivalent evidence), and must not synthesize user answers that were never provided.
- actual: Intake evidence can state required topics were asked and answered despite no user-facing question round, producing misleading `asked_topics`/`topic_coverage` records.
- evidence_refs: user report (2026-04-03); example file `handoffs/intake_evidence/BUG-0006-intake-20260403.json`; this intake bundle `handoffs/intake_evidence/BUG-0007-intake-20260403.json`; intake contract `.cursor/commands/intake.md`.
- intake_notes (2026-04-03, PO, manual): Captured as intake evidence integrity defect. Scope is truthful asked-vs-covered accounting and fail-closed behavior when required topics were not actually collected from the user.

### BUG-0008 — Global Linux install fails: empty `install_include_paths` when manifest is CRLF
- user_visible: true
- Status: DONE
- environment: Debian Linux (Docker host `docker-dmz`); global npm **`its-magic@0.1.2-40`**; **`/usr/lib/node_modules/its-magic/template/docs/engineering/context/installer-owned-paths.manifest`** shows **`^M$`** per **`cat -A`**.
- steps_to_reproduce: 1. **`npm install -g its-magic`** (package built/published with CRLF manifest lines). 2. Run **`its-magic --target <repo> --mode missing`** (or equivalent). 3. Observe **`[INSTALL_MANIFEST_ERROR] install_include_paths section is empty`** despite visible path lines in the manifest file.
- expected: **`installer.sh`** **`get_manifest_paths`** parses **`[install_include_paths]`** and lists paths under LF or CRLF; prepublish guard rejects CR in manifest; **`.gitattributes`** keeps **`*.manifest`** LF in git/npm.
- actual: POSIX **`awk`** compares **`$0 == "[install_include_paths]"`**; trailing **`\\r`** prevents match → empty section → install aborts.
- evidence_refs: operator **`npm ls -g its-magic`** + **`sed -n '1,25p' ... | cat -A`** (2026-04-04); intake bundle **`handoffs/intake_evidence/BUG-0008-intake-20260404.json`**; **`python scripts/intake_evidence_validate.py --file handoffs/intake_evidence/BUG-0008-intake-20260404.json`** → **`[INTAKE_EVIDENCE_VALIDATION_OK]`**.
- intake_notes (2026-04-04, PO, Cursor): Bounded defect; fix = defensive **`sub(/\\r$/, \"\")`** in manifest awk + **`*.manifest text eol=lf`** + **`guard_installer_publish`** CR check on both manifest copies; harness **26P2**; **installer.ps1** **`TrimEnd('`r')`** for **`Get-ManifestSection`**; normalized in-repo **`docs/`** + **`template/`** manifests to LF (removed stray **`\\r`**).
- Triad hot-surface (**DEC-0054**): **`enforce-triad-hot-surface.py --rollover`** twice — **`po-to-tl-pack-20260404-c.md`** (full **BUG-0008** handoff), **`po-to-tl-pack-20260404-d.md`** (compact **BUG-0008** pointer); hot **`po_to_tl.md`** ends at **800** lines; **`--check`** **PASS** (latest **BUG-0008** pointer text in **`pack-20260404-d.md`**).
- discovery_notes (2026-04-04T20:00:00Z, po, `fresh_context_marker=po-BUG0008-discovery-20260404T200000Z-fresh`): **`/discovery`** **PASS** — scope matches **R-0069** (CRLF → **`awk`** section header mismatch → empty **`install_include_paths`**). **In-repo** mitigations satisfy backlog **expected** for source + runtime: **`installer.sh`** **`get_manifest_paths`** strips trailing **`\\r`**; **`.gitattributes`** **`*.manifest text eol=lf`**; **`scripts/guard_installer_publish.py`** rejects **`\\r`** in active + template manifests; **`tests/installer_manifest_crlf_bug0008_test.py`** exercises CRLF fixture; **installer.ps1** parity per **intake_notes**. **Remaining work** (not discovery gaps): **npm republish** so operators leave broken **`its-magic@0.1.2-40`** tarball; **Debian global E2E** per intake **done_definition** (**`cat -A`**, **`its-magic --mode missing`** without **`[INSTALL_MANIFEST_ERROR]`**); close **R-0069** on delivery. **Open for TL**: release/version bump sequencing vs **US-0084**; confirm **run-tests** harness registration (**26P2** / intake) and whether **verify-work** needs explicit post-publish global-install smoke. **Next recommended phase: `/architecture`** — **R-0069** already locks root cause and mitigations; TL should define sprint/AC/tasks + publish/QA gates (**not** **`/research`** repeat). **Status remains OPEN** (**US-0045**; no QA/release closure yet).

- related_us: US-0084

### BUG-0009 — its-magic ships its OWN packaging CI into generated repos, breaking CI in every created project
- user_visible: true
- Status: DONE
- environment: Any repository created/installed with `its-magic` (reported across all such repos; concrete example `finance_goblin` on GitHub Actions, Ubuntu + Windows runners). Affected file copied into targets: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (from packaged `template/.github/workflows/ci.yml`). Installer copy path declared in `docs/engineering/context/installer-owned-paths.manifest` (`[install_include_paths]` `.github/workflows`; `[clean_paths]` `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`).
- steps_to_reproduce: 1. Install its-magic into a new project (`its-magic --mode missing` / `--create`) or upgrade an existing one. 2. Push to GitHub so the copied `.github/workflows/ci.yml` runs. 3. Observe the `npm-test`, `brew-test`, and `choco-test` jobs fail in the target repo even though the project is not its-magic itself.
- expected: A generated/installed project receives only generic, project-agnostic CI (the runbook-driven `checks` + `auto-fix` jobs). its-magic's own distribution/self-packaging jobs (`npm pack its-magic-*.tgz`, `sh installer.sh`, `packaging/chocolatey` choco pack, `packaging/homebrew` brew style) must NOT be copied into downstream repos, and must not run there. New installs/upgrades produce a CI workflow that is green-by-default for a fresh project, and the generic `checks` job does not hard-fail merely because a fresh project has no real tests/lint configured yet.
- actual: `template/.github/workflows/ci.yml` is byte-identical to its-magic's own `.github/workflows/ci.yml` and includes the self-packaging jobs `npm-test` (`npm pack` → expects `its-magic-*.tgz` / `package.json`), `brew-test` (`sh installer.sh ...`), and `choco-test` (`Push-Location packaging/chocolatey; choco pack`). None of those paths exist in a downstream project, so the workflow fails in every its-magic-created repo: `npm error enoent Could not read package.json` (exit 254); `sh: installer.sh: No such file or directory` (exit 127); `Push-Location : Cannot find path '...\packaging\chocolatey'` (exit 1). Additionally the generic `checks` job can fail with `Tests or lint failed` for fresh projects with no real tests/lint.
- evidence_refs: user report (2026-06-06) with GitHub Actions logs from `finance_goblin` (`npm pack` ENOENT package.json exit 254; `sh installer.sh` No such file exit 127; `Push-Location packaging/chocolatey` PathNotFound exit 1; `Tests or lint failed` exit 1); `template/.github/workflows/ci.yml` (jobs `npm-test`, `brew-test`, `choco-test`); `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (identical source); `docs/engineering/context/installer-owned-paths.manifest`; `installer.ps1`; `installer.sh`; `installer.py`; `handoffs/intake_evidence/BUG-0009-intake-20260606.json` (`python scripts/intake_evidence_validate.py --file handoffs/intake_evidence/BUG-0009-intake-20260606.json` → `[INTAKE_EVIDENCE_VALIDATION_OK]`).
- intake_notes (2026-06-06, PO, Cursor, `cursor-20260606-BUG0009-intake`, `/intake bug`, `INTAKE_GUIDED_MODE=1`): Captured as a packaging/template-leak defect. Root cause hypothesis (for `/discovery` to confirm): the active repo's CI and the downstream template CI were kept identical, so its-magic-only distribution jobs (`npm-test`/`brew-test`/`choco-test`) leak into every generated repo. No CI-parity guard currently enforces `template/.github/workflows/ci.yml == .github/workflows/ci.yml` (only intake-script parity is guarded via `scripts/check_intake_template_parity.py`), so the template CI can be safely decoupled. Operator scope decisions (asked this run): (1) fix targets new installs/upgrades only — existing broken repos heal on their next its-magic upgrade since `upgrade`/`clean` modes re-copy the corrected `ci.yml`; (2) also include hardening so the generic `checks` job is tolerant/clear when a fresh project has no real tests yet. Proposed fix direction (defer exact design to architecture): split CI into a downstream-safe template workflow (generic `checks` + `auto-fix` only, runbook-driven, no self-packaging jobs) vs its-magic's own internal CI (keep `npm-test`/`brew-test`/`choco-test` for self-distribution), and add a regression/drift guard that fails if the template CI references its-magic self-packaging paths. **Status: OPEN** per **US-0045**. **Next**: `/discovery` (fresh PO context) for `BUG-0009`.
